## Analyzer Architecture Summary

mcp-lifecycle-module-operator (also known as\: MCP Lifecycle Module operator, MCP Lifecycle operator) is a Kubernetes component. managing 1 CRD(s)\: MCPLifecycleOperator. deploying 5 workload(s) (controller-manager, controller-manager, controller-manager, controller-manager, controller-manager) with 5 container(s). watching 11 resource type(s). exposing 1 service(s) on ports\: 443. defining 1 API spec type(s) across 8 struct(s) with 23 total fields. with 67 operator config constants (2 image references). following 1 reconciliation steps across 1 controller(s). reporting 3 status condition types. with 1 platform-conditional resource paths. with 2 ingress route(s). integrates with management relationship via labels/annotations. creates RBAC bindings to external roles\: mcp-lifecycle-module-operator-manager-role. kustomize overlays\: config/crd, config/default, config/manager, config/manifests/mcp-lifecycle-operator/certmanager, config/manifests/mcp-lifecycle-operator/coverage, config/manifests/mcp-lifecycle-operator/crd, config/manifests/mcp-lifecycle-operator/default, config/manifests/mcp-lifecycle-operator/default-with-webhook, config/manifests/mcp-lifecycle-operator/e2e-restricted, config/manifests/mcp-lifecycle-operator/manager, config/manifests/mcp-lifecycle-operator/manager-debug, config/manifests/mcp-lifecycle-operator/network-policy, config/manifests/mcp-lifecycle-operator/overlays/odh, config/manifests/mcp-lifecycle-operator/prometheus, config/manifests/mcp-lifecycle-operator/rbac, config/manifests/mcp-lifecycle-operator/storage-migration, config/manifests/mcp-lifecycle-operator/webhook, config/network-policy, config/rbac.
